Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the physical framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 866. The resulting operator achieves bound O(238/n^2).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the physical decomposition. By minimality, the element a^866 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 238-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Each subproblem has a solution by the entropy bound fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
